payon,164,229,5	script	Elder Sanyul#illusion_moonlight	1_F_ORIENT_04,{
	if (BaseLevel < 100) {
		mes "[ 山裕 ]";
		mes "嗯，我感觉有可疑的事情要发生了。";
		mes "希望你能更强一些。那样我就能告诉你了。";
		next;
		mes "^4D4DFF你必须达到100级或更高才能进行此任务。^000000";
		close;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 0) {
	OnStart:
		mes "[ 佳严 ]";
		mes "这件事已经超出我们的控制。";
		mes "不能再让事情这样继续下去。";
		next;
		mes "[ 山裕 ]";
		mes "现在等^0000FF沐妍^000000吧。";
		mes "她的安全比什么都重要。";
		next;
		select("打扰一下。" );
		mes "[ 山裕 ]";
		mes "嗯？你是冒险者。";
		mes "你碰巧听到了我们的谈话吗？";
		next;
		mes "[ 山裕 ]";
		mes "我明白了。";
		mes "也许是命运让你听到我们的话。";
		next;
		mes "[ 山裕 ]";
		mes "在斐扬后方的弓箭手村附近有一座洞穴。";
		mes "那是一座闹鬼的洞穴，所以我们一直定期在那里举行仪式和驱魔。";
		next;
		mes "[ 山裕 ]";
		mes "但九尾狐的灵魂比我们想象的更加愤怒。";
		mes "每当有人试图对狐珠举行仪式，就会做噩梦。";
		npctalk "仿佛那些灵魂被仪式激怒了。","Elder Jagyeom#illusion_moonlight",bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[ 山裕 ]";
		mes "而这一次，她完全失去理智，进入了洞穴。";
		mes "所以我们很担心她。";
		next;
		select("是关于沐妍吗？" );
		npctalk "也许我们应该派别人去。","Elder Jagyeom#illusion_moonlight",bc_self;
		mes "[ 山裕 ]";
		mes "是的。她是定期举行仪式的灵术师。";
		mes "我们已经让一名士兵去找她。";
		mes "他们现在应该已经到洞穴附近了。";
		next;
		mes "[ 佳严 ]";
		mes "你能替我们去看看^0000FF沐妍^000000吗？";
		next;
		npctalk "可以。你能去吗？","",bc_self;
		if (select("好，我去。：抱歉，我怕鬼。") == 2) {
			mes "[ 山裕 ]";
			mes "嗯？我们没说过鬼。还是说过？";
			close;
		}
		mes "[ 佳严 ]";
		mes "希望士兵能在她深入废墟村落前找到她。";
		next;
		mes "[ 佳严 ]";
		mes "请去洞穴看看沐妍和士兵是否已经在外面。";
		mes "一名年轻学者跟着士兵去了，但她看起来完全不可靠。";
		next;
		mes "[ 佳严 ]";
		mes "和那名学者谈谈，也许他能告诉你情况。";
		illusion_moonlight = 1;
		setquest 7776;
		close;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 1) {
		mes "[ 佳严 ]";
		mes "希望士兵能在她深入废墟村落前找到她。";
		next;
		mes "[ 佳严 ]";
		mes "请去洞穴看看沐妍和士兵是否已经在外面。";
		mes "一名年轻学者跟着士兵去了，但她看起来完全不可靠。";
		next;
		mes "[ 佳严 ]";
		mes "和那名学者谈谈，也许他能告诉你情况。";
		close;
	}
	mes "[ 山裕 ]";
	mes "希望一切顺利。";
	mes "嗯？我说的是后面的洞穴。";
	mes "我很担心那里发生的事。";
	close;

OnInit:
	questinfo QTYPE_QUEST,QMARK_YELLOW,"BaseLevel >= 100 && illusion_moonlight < 1";
	end;
}

payon,167,229,3	script	Elder Jagyeom#illusion_moonlight	4_M_HUOLDARMY,{
	if (BaseLevel < 100) {
		mes "[ 佳严 ]";
		mes "希望一切顺利。";
		mes "既然你是冒险者，我想你应该熟悉^0000FF斐扬洞穴^000000。";
		mes "小心，里面正在发生不好的事情。";
		next;
		mes "^0000FF你必须达到100级或更高才能进行此任务。";
		close;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 0) {
		doevent "Elder Sanyul#illusion_moonlight::OnStart";
		end;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 1) {
		mes "[ 山裕 ]";
		mes "请前往弓箭手村的洞穴入口。";
		mes "询问那里的年轻学者沐妍的下落。";
		close;
	}
		mes "[ 佳严 ]";
	mes "也许是时候把事情交给年轻一代了。";
	mes "我们这些老人什么也做不了。";
	close;
}

pay_arche,48,137,4	script	Young Scholar#ill_moon_arch	4_F_03,{
	if (illusion_moonlight == 0) {
			npctalk "他们怎么还不出来……","",bc_self;
		end;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 1) {
		npctalk "他们怎么还不出来……","",bc_self;
		mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
		mes "啊，今天一定是我的幸运日！";
		mes "我正需要冒险者的时候，冒险者就出现了！";
		next;
		mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
		mes "打扰一下。";
		mes "我听说冒险者会为别人做很多事。是真的吗？";
		next;
		select("是的，但我现在很忙。：拒绝。" );
		mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
		mes "……哦，我明白了。";
		mes "你一定很忙。";
		mes "抱歉打扰你。";
		mes "对了，你为什么来这里？";
		next;
		select("你见过一个叫沐妍的人吗？" );
		mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
		mes "沐妍？你说的是灵术师沐妍吗？";
		mes "严格来说她还是学徒，不过这不重要。";
		next;
		mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
		mes "我现在正在等她。";
		mes "她像被什么附身一样跑进了洞穴。";
		next;
		mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
		mes "正熙？我是说，一名士兵跟着她进去了，但他们都还没出来。";
		mes "我正在犹豫要不要跟进去。";
		next;
		mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
		mes "希望那两个人不要出事。";
		cloakoffnpcself( "Payon Soldier#ill_moon_arch" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Muyeon#ill_moon_arch" );
		sleep2 750;
		npctalk "他们在那里！看起来没事！","Young Scholar#ill_moon_arch",bc_self;
		sleep2 1500;
		npctalk "呃，我不想再训练了！","Muyeon#ill_moon_arch",bc_self;
		npctalk "……","Payon Soldier#ill_moon_arch",bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "正熙，你在外面做什么？";
		npctalk "我很担心你……","",bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "你担心我，却只愿意跟到这里吗？";
		mes "算了。不管我多么痛苦，也不需要你的帮助！";
		sleep2 500;
		npctalk "好了，够了。","Payon Soldier#ill_moon_arch",bc_self;
		sleep2 500;
		npctalk "这、这不公平……","",bc_self;
		next;
		npctalk "再见！","Muyeon#ill_moon_arch",bc_self;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "啊，我再也不做这个了。把这话告诉那些老家伙！";
		next;
		npctalk "唉。我去追她。你能和长老们谈谈吗？","Payon Soldier#ill_moon_arch",bc_self;
		sleep2 1500;
		cloakonnpcself( "Payon Soldier#ill_moon_arch" );
		cloakonnpcself( "Muyeon#ill_moon_arch" );
		mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
		mes "那是……！";
		mes "她什么也没告诉我们就走了。";
		next;
		mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
		mes "啊，正熙！";
		mes "对了，冒险者，你不是在找沐妍吗？";
		next;
		select("我想我现在该走了。" );
		mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
		mes "沐妍一定去了^0000CD独立斐扬宫殿^000000。";
		mes "我要去那里，你要和我一起吗？";
		next;
		illusion_moonlight = 2;
		erasequest 7776;
		setquest 7777;
		if (select("和他一起去。：分开走。") == 2) {
			mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
			mes "沐妍一定去了^0000CD独立斐扬宫殿^000000。";
			mes "你去和她谈谈吧。";
			mes "我去和长老们谈谈。";
			close;
        }
		mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
		mes "那么我们去^0000CD独立斐扬宫殿^000000吧。";
        close2;
        warp "payon",105,320;
        end;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 2) {
		mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
		mes "沐妍一定去了^0000CD独立斐扬宫殿^000000。";
		mes "你去和她谈谈吧。";
		mes "我去和长老们谈谈。";
		close;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight < 6) {
		mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
		mes "传说九尾狐获得启蒙，成为神圣生物，月光花就是从她身上降生的。我认为这个故事值得调查。";
		close;
	}
	mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
	mes "我们去调查废墟村落吧！";
	mes "你要和我一起去吗？";
	next;
	if (select("和他一起去。：分开走。") == 2) {
		mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
		mes "我会叫上正熙，很快就跟上你。";
		close;
	}
	mes "[ 年轻学者 ]";
	mes "走吧！";
	close2;
	warp "pay_dun03",145,43;
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo QTYPE_QUEST,QMARK_YELLOW,"illusion_moonlight == 1";
	end;
}

pay_arche,44,133,7	duplicate(dummy_cloaked_npc)	Payon Soldier#ill_moon_arch	4_M_PAY_SOLDIER
pay_arche,46,135,5	duplicate(dummy_cloaked_npc)	Muyeon#ill_moon_arch	4_F_TAEKWON

payon_in03,33,95,3	script	Muyeon#ill_moon_pin	4_F_TAEKWON,{
	if (checkweight(25271,1) == 0 || (MaxWeight - Weight) < 1000) {
		mes "- 你携带的物品太多，无法继续任务。-";
		close;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight < 2) {
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "如果你在找老师，她在另一间房。";
		close;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 2) {
		.@elder$ = "Jagyeom#ill_moon_pin";
		.@scholar$ = "Young Scholar#ill_moon_pin";
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_ALL,true;
		npctalk "不管你说什么，我都不再做了。","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2500;
		npctalk "这是我第三次做噩梦了。我再也受不了了！","",bc_self;
		sleep2 1000;
		unittalk getcharid(3),"什么噩梦？",bc_self;
		npctalk "什么噩梦？",.@scholar$,bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "关于狐狸的噩梦？九尾和月光花？","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "那些噩梦太可怕了。我不想再经历了。","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "但已经没有其他人能举行仪式了。",.@elder$,bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "大家第一次尝试后就放弃了。你已经做过三次。",.@elder$,bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "佳严长老，这次发生的不只是噩梦。","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "和我前两次举行仪式时不同。","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "有多不同？",.@scholar$,bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "...","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "感觉噩梦正在现实中发生。","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "你是什么意思？你只是想让愤怒的九尾狐灵魂得到安宁。",.@elder$,bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "你在洞穴里看到了什么？",.@elder$,bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "我无法解释看到的东西。你必须亲眼看见才会相信。","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "需要强者进去，比如那边的冒险者，应该让他进洞穴。","",bc_self;
		illusion_moonlight = 3;
		completequest 7777;
		setquest 7778;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_ALL,false;
		end;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 3) {
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "好，我来告诉你故事。";
		mes "斐扬洞穴曾经是一个村庄的一部分，后来被山体滑坡掩埋。";
		next;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "你可以猜到，许多人也被埋在其中，他们的灵魂在洞穴中游荡。";
		next;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "其中有九尾狐。狐狸的愤怒非同寻常。";
		mes "所以我们一直定期举行仪式。";
		next;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "但现在问题是，";
		mes "狐狸的灵魂越来越怨恨，却没人知道原因。";
		mes "也许仪式正是它们愤怒的原因，没有像我们希望的那样奏效。";
		next;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "每次举行仪式，我都会梦到怨恨的灵魂和哀嚎的九尾狐。";
		next;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "灵术师以和灵魂交流为生，做噩梦是很正常的事。";
		next;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "所以第一次做噩梦时，我以为和其他人一样。";
		mes "但现在我知道其中的区别了，这也与我的健康无关。";
		next;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "无论是什么原因激怒了九尾狐的灵魂，";
		mes "它都让我失去理智，前往废墟村落。";
		next;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "而且，我看到了某种东西。";
		mes "那是另一个世界的影像，和我们的世界不同。";
		mes "我们必须关闭那个地方，让任何人都无法进入。";
		next;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "我有种不安的感觉，似乎可怕的事情即将发生。";
		mes "或者它已经发生了……";
		next;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "我看到的模糊影像与地狱无异。";
		mes "那是在我最后一次举行仪式后看到的。";
		next;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "求你了，别再让我回去做那件事。";
		mes "我的精神还不够坚强，无法承受这样的噩梦。";
		next;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "更不用说洞穴里正在发生的事情。";
		next;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "如果你想去，就必须独自前往，亲眼看看。";
		mes "我还带着试图净化的狐珠。准备好后告诉我。";
		illusion_moonlight = 4;
		completequest 7778;
		setquest 7779;
		close;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 4) {
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "准备好了吗？";
		next;
		if (select("好了。：还没。") == 2) {
			mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
			mes "准备好后告诉我。";
			close;
		}
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "好，这颗狐珠还没有完全净化。";
		mes "把它放在手里。";
		next;
		mes "[ 沐妍 ]";
		mes "放松……集中精神……向它的能量敞开心扉。";
		close2;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_ALL,true;
		npctalk "游荡的灵魂，听着并见证吧！","",bc_self;
		sleep2 250;
		specialeffect EF_PORTAL4;
		specialeffect EF_GROUNDSAMPLE;
		sleep2 1800;
		npctalk "看冥界河的另一边。","",bc_self;
		sleep2 250;
		specialeffect EF_PORTAL5;
		specialeffect EF_QUAKEBODY2;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < 4; .@i++) {
			cloakoffnpcself( "Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_" + .@i );
			specialeffect EF_REDBODY,AREA,"Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_" + .@i;
			specialeffect EF_QUAKEBODY2,AREA,"Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_" + .@i;
			sleep2 250;
		}
		sleep2 1500;
		npctalk "可怜的灵魂，捂住眼睛，堵住耳朵。","",bc_self;
		sleep2 150;
		specialeffect2 EF_DEVIL1;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < 4; .@i++) {
			specialeffect EF_QUAKEBODY2,AREA,"Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_" + .@i;
			sleep2 30;
		}
		npctalk "去死！","Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_0",bc_self;
		sleep2 100;
		specialeffect2 EF_DEVIL6;
		sleep2 100;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < 4; .@i++) {
			specialeffect EF_QUAKEBODY2,AREA,"Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_" + .@i;
			sleep2 30;
		}
		sleep2 150;
		npctalk "去死！","Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_1",bc_self;
		sleep2 1000;
		npctalk "离开这个领域。","",bc_self;
		sleep2 150;
		specialeffect2 EF_DEVIL10;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < 4; .@i++) {
			specialeffect EF_QUAKEBODY2,AREA,"Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_" + .@i;
			sleep2 30;
		}
		npctalk "去死！","Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_2",bc_self;
		sleep2 150;
		specialeffect EF_BLUELINE;
		sleep2 150;
		npctalk "去死！","Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_3",bc_self;
		sleep2 700;
		npctalk "卸下你的负担。","",bc_self;
		sleep2 150;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < 4; .@i++) {
			specialeffect EF_MAP_GHOST,AREA,"Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_" + .@i;
			sleep2 30;
		}
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < 4; .@i++)
			npctalk "去死！！！","Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_" + .@i,bc_self;
		sleep2 1200;
		specialeffect EF_BLUELINE;
		sleep2 500;
		specialeffect EF_SCREEN_QUAKE;
		sleep2 1200;
		npctalk "飞到河的另一边。","",bc_self;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < 4; .@i++) {
			specialeffect EF_MADNESS_RED,AREA,"Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_" + .@i;
			sleep2 30;
		}
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < 4; .@i++) {
			npctalk "去死！", "Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_" + .@i, bc_self;
			sleep2 50;
			specialeffect EF_SCREEN_QUAKE;
			sleep2 300;
		}
		sleep2 1000;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < 4; .@i++) {
			npctalk "去死！", "Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_" + .@i, bc_self;
			sleep2 100;
		}
		specialeffect2 EF_JUMPBODY1;
		sleep2 150;
		completequest 7779;
		setquest 7780;

		mes "请消灭^0000cd静谧亡者^000000。它们有^0000cd小型、快速型和大型三种，请各消灭10只。^000000";
		next;
		mes "[Gem]";
		mes "所有死者都必须回归大地。";
		close;
	case 2:
		mes "[Gem]";
		mes "冒险者，你把它们全都处理好了。谢谢。它们也会在天堂感谢你。";
		next;
		mes "[Gem]";
		mes "你去休息一下，天亮后再回来吧？";
		erasequest 14667;// Send the Dead Back to Earth
		setquest 14668;// Get Some Rest
		getexp 500000,500000;
		getitem 25271,1;// Illusion Stone
		close;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	.@main_completed$ = "isbegin_quest(14666) == 2";
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(14656) == 1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(14665) == 1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, (.@main_completed$ + " && checkquest(14668,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(14667,HUNTING) == -1") );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, (.@main_completed$ + " && checkquest(14667,HUNTING) == 2") );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, (.@main_completed$ + " && checkquest(14668,PLAYTIME) == 2") );
	end;
}

		if (mobcount("gef_d01_i", "ill_vampire_spawn_dracula::OnMobDead") < 1) {
			mes "[Bomi]";

		}
		else {
			mes "[" + .@player_name$ + "]";

			.@npc_name$ = "Bomi's Note";
		}
		next;
		mes "[" + .@npc_name$ + "]";

		next;
		mes "[" + .@npc_name$ + "]";
mes "谢谢你的帮助。";
		erasequest 14674;// Time to Digest
		setquest 14671;// Unreliable Fake Blood
		close;
	}
	switch( checkquest(14671,HUNTING) ) {
	case -1:
		//if (mobcount("gef_d01_i", "ill_vampire_spawn_dracula::OnMobDead") > 0) {// unknown text

mes "冒险者，你已经报告过了吗？还要在这里做别的事吗？";
		next;
		mes "[Bomi]";
mes "如果你不忙，能帮我做件事吗？";
		next;
		if (select( "当然。", "我现在有点忙。") == 2) {
			mes "[Bomi]";
mes "哦，是吗？抱歉。也许你可以晚点再来？不，算了。";
			close;
		}

		close;
	}
	end;

OnTouch:
	if (ill_vampire == 7) {
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, true;
		npctalk "这个和霓虹蘑菇的颜色不同。是新品种吗？我最好小心地采摘……", "", bc_self;
		sleep2 1000;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
		unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + "：典型的巫师……等等，那会是乔乔吗？？", bc_self;
	}
	else if (ill_vampire == 12 || ill_vampire == 13) {
		cloakoffnpcself( "Gem#ilgf02" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Marina#ilgf02" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Antoine#ilgf02" );
		if (ill_vampire == 13) {
			cloakoffnpcself( "Dracula#ilgf03" );
			cloakoffnpcself( "King#ilgf02" );
			cloakoffnpcself( "Jojo#ilgf01" );
		}
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	// todo: missing questinfo ?

	.@main_completed$ = "isbegin_quest(14666) == 2";
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, (.@main_completed$ + " && checkquest(14674,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(14671,HUNTING) == -1") );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, (.@main_completed$ + " && checkquest(14671,HUNTING) == 2 && countitem(25268) > 9 && countitem(25269) > 9") );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, (.@main_completed$ + " && checkquest(14674,PLAYTIME) == 2") );

	if (strnpcinfo(0) == "Wizard#ilgf001")
		disablenpc();
	end;
}

		next;
		mes "[Dracula]";

		npctalk "你该停止和人类混在一起。你现在听起来就像他们。", "King#ilgf01", bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[King]";

		npctalk "你为什么还要坚持留在这种地方？", "King#ilgf01", bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[Dracula]";
		mes "我不是吗？";
		next;
		emotion ET_SURPRISE, getnpcid(0, "King#ilgf01");
		mes "[King]";
		mes "大家安静。我闻到什么了。";
		erasequest 14661;// Jojo Is the Mage's Name 02
		setquest 14662;// You're Scaring Me
		ill_vampire = 11;
		close;
	}
	if (ill_vampire == 11) {
		mes "[Dracula]";
		mes "国王？是什么？";
		close;
	}
	if (ill_vampire == 12 || ill_vampire == 13) {
		mes "[Dracula]";
		mes "战斗？糟糕，我们得快点！";
		close2;
		viewpoint 1,85,131,1,0x00FF00;
		end;
	}
	if (ill_vampire == 14 || ill_vampire == 15) {
		mes "[Dracula]";
		mes "我决定等她。反正我也需要休息。";
		close;
	}
	mes "[Dracula]";
	mes "她喝的蝙蝠血有时会让她失去理智。过一会儿她会平静下来，但如果教会的人看到她脆弱的状态，就会试图杀死她。";
	close;
}


//============================================================
//= Illusion of Frozen
//============================================================
// boss summoning - 1 day of delay for now
ice_d03_i,126,126,3	script	Orb of Seal#frozen1	CLEAR_NPC,{
	if (.ill_frozen_boss_timer >= gettimetick(2)) {
		mes "这个宝珠还需要时间聚集足够能量才能运作。";
		close;
	}
	.@i = atoi( charat(strnpcinfo(2), 6) );
	.timer[.@i] = 30;
	initnpctimer;	// the effect continue after boss spawn
	if (.timer[1] > 0 && .timer[2] > 0 && .timer[3] > 0 && .timer[4] > 0) {
		.ill_frozen_boss_timer = gettimetick(2) + 86400;// respawn timer (seems to be one day)
		donpcevent "bosseff#frozen::OnEvent";
	}
	end;
OnTimer1000:
	.@i = atoi( charat(strnpcinfo(2), 6) );
	.timer[.@i]--;
	if (.timer[.@i] < 1)
		stopnpctimer;
	else {
		initnpctimer;
		specialeffect EF_DETOXICATION;
	}
	end;
}
ice_d03_i,172,126,3	duplicate(Orb of Seal#frozen1)	Orb of Seal#frozen2	CLEAR_NPC
ice_d03_i,172,173,3	duplicate(Orb of Seal#frozen1)	Orb of Seal#frozen3	CLEAR_NPC
ice_d03_i,127,172,3	duplicate(Orb of Seal#frozen1)	Orb of Seal#frozen4	CLEAR_NPC

ice_d03_i,149,112,0	script	bosseff#frozen	HIDDEN_WARP_NPC,{
	end;
OnEvent:
	initnpctimer;
	end;
OnTimer1000:
	mapannounce "ice_d03_i", "洞穴中封印的全部能量已经释放，唤醒了 Ktullanux。", bc_map, 0xFFFF00;
	end;
OnTimer3000:
	stopnpctimer;
	mapannounce "ice_d03_i", "Ktullanux: Who dares call me a lizard? I'll freeze you all!", bc_map, 0xFFFF00;
	specialeffect EF_PORTAL;
	if (mobcount("ice_d03_i", "bosseff#frozen::OnBossDead") < 1)
		monster "ice_d03_i",149,112,"Awakened Ktullanux",3796,1, "bosseff#frozen::OnBossDead";	// ILL_KTULLANUX
	end;
OnBossDead:
	mapannounce "ice_d03_i", "Ktullanux: I'll... be... back...", bc_map;
	end;
}

ice_d03_i,152,43,0	script	cine1#frozen	HIDDEN_WARP_NPC,13,13,{
	end;
OnTouch:
	if (checkquest(15115,PLAYTIME) == 2) {
		illusion_frozen = 0;	// reset the variable for daily quests
		erasequest 15115;// Vrid and Zeith
	}
	.@char_id = getcharid(0);
	if (illusion_frozen < 3) {
		cloakoffnpcself( "Zeith#frozen01" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Vrid#frozen01" );
		end;
	}
	if (illusion_frozen == 3) {
		cloakoffnpcself( "Vrid#frozen01" );
		if (checkquest(15112,HUNTING) == 2)
			cloakoffnpcself( "Zeith#frozen02" );
		end;
	}
	if (illusion_frozen == 4) {
		cloakoffnpcself( "Zeith#frozen02" );
		if (checkquest(15113,HUNTING) == 2)
			cloakoffnpcself( "Vrid#frozen02" );
		end;
	}
	end;
}

		}
		close;
	}
	end;

	stopnpctimer;
	enablenpc();
	end;
}

		unittalk .@account_id, "" + .@player_name$ + "：这是什么意思？那里看起来不像普通的海龟岛？", bc_self;
		sleep2 1500;
		npctalk "艾尔瓦：很难解释，那里明显是海龟岛，但我却起了一身鸡皮疙瘩。", "", bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[Knight Aylvar]";
		mes "我当时身处险境，但有人救了我。";
		next;
		mes "[Knight Aylvar]";

		next;
		mes "[Knight Aylvar]";

		next;
		if (select( "你的朋友遇到麻烦了吗？", "难以置信。" ) == 2) {

		}
		else {
			emotion ET_PROFUSELY_SWAT;
			mes "[Knight Aylvar]";

		}
		next;
		npctalk "莱芬：我是莱芬，朱诺的考古学教授。", "", bc_self;

		mes "[Knight Aylvar]";

		next;
		select("请告诉我你朋友的长相。");

		next;

		next;
		if (select( "是的。我要去那里。", "现在还不去。" ) == 2) {

			completequest 16114;	// Talk with Aylvar
			close;
		}
		mes "[Knight Aylvar]";
		mes "请再回来，" + .@player_name$ + "。";
		mes "路上请确认莱芬一切安好。";
		ill_turtle = 12;
		setquest 16114;	// Talk with Aylvar
		completequest 16114;	// Talk with Aylvar
		close2;
		warp "tur_d03_i",132,187;
		end;
	}
	npctalk "艾尔瓦：对他来说研究比自己更重要，所以我想待在他身边。", "", bc_self;
	mes "[Knight Aylvar]";
	mes "很高兴再次见到你。我已经把情报报告给骑士团，现在的工作是支持莱芬的研究。";

	if (select( "是的。我要去那里。", "现在还不去。" ) == 2) {

		mes "听完他的故事后，我也想去那里……但我无法像他说的那样前往。真遗憾。";
		close;
	}
	mes "[Knight Aylvar]";
	mes "请再回来，" + .@player_name$ + "。";
	mes "如果找到莱芬，请确认他有好好吃饭。";
	close2;
	emotion ET_KEK;
	warp "tur_d03_i",132,187;
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle == 0 && BaseLevel > 149" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle == 11" );
	end;
}

	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle == 9" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle == 10" );

	// daily
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle > 10 && checkquest(16105,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle > 10 && checkquest(16108,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle > 10 && isbegin_quest(16106) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle > 10 && isbegin_quest(16106) == 0 && checkquest(16105,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(16108,PLAYTIME) == -1" );
	end;
}

		next;
		mes "[Girl]";
		mes "你第一次遇见我的地方……记得吗？我会在那里等你。";
		next;
		npctalk "不见了。那些活人。", "Rancorous Ghost#illtur05", bc_self;
		sleep2 1000;
		specialeffect EF_DANCE1, AREA, "Rancorous Ghost#illtur03";
		cloakonnpcself( "Rancorous Ghost#illtur03" );
		npctalk "不，我的孩子……", "Rancorous Ghost#illtur03", bc_self;
		sleep2 1000;
		specialeffect EF_DANCE1, AREA, "Rancorous Ghost#illtur07";
		cloakonnpcself( "Rancorous Ghost#illtur07" );
		npctalk "这里的所有人都死了。", "Rancorous Ghost#illtur07", bc_self;
		sleep2 1000;
		specialeffect EF_DANCE1, AREA, "Rancorous Ghost#illtur04";
		cloakonnpcself( "Rancorous Ghost#illtur04" );
		npctalk "我的宝藏……", "Rancorous Ghost#illtur04", bc_self;
		sleep2 1000;
		specialeffect EF_DANCE1, AREA, "Rancorous Ghost#illtur05";
		cloakonnpcself( "Rancorous Ghost#illtur05" );
		npctalk "我们回去吧。", "Girl#illtur02", bc_self;
		sleep2 1000;
		cloakonnpcself( "Girl#illtur02" );
		npctalk "我饿了……好饿。", "Rancorous Ghost#illtur02", bc_self;
		sleep2 1000;
		specialeffect EF_DANCE1, AREA, "Rancorous Ghost#illtur02";
		cloakonnpcself( "Rancorous Ghost#illtur02" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Fragile Woman#illtur" );
		npctalk "和往常一样。", "Rancorous Ghost#illtur01", bc_self;
		sleep2 1000;
		specialeffect EF_DANCE1, AREA, "Rancorous Ghost#illtur01";
		cloakonnpcself( "Rancorous Ghost#illtur01" );
		npctalk "全都死了……", "Rancorous Ghost#illtur06", bc_self;
		sleep2 1000;
		cloakonnpcself( "Rancorous Ghost#illtur06" );
		sleep2 3000;
		unittalk .@account_id, "" + .@player_name$ + " : We don't have a choice, just believe her. Let's go upward!", bc_self;

		next;
		mes " - ^424242Archeologist Leiphen held his breath and nodded.^000000 - ";

		erasequest 16103;	// The Secret of Strangers
		setquest 16104;	// A long-forgotten story
		close2;
		cloakonnpcself( "Archeologist Leiphen#illtur03" );
		end;
	}
	if (ill_turtle > 10) {
		switch( checkquest(16105,PLAYTIME) ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "[Fragile Woman]";
			mes "我饿了……好饿。我要死了。";
			next;
			mes "[Fragile Woman]";
			mes "眼前的东西我什么都能吃。从头到脚……";
			next;
			mes " - ^424242I should be closer. After dawn, maybe calmer.";
			close2;
			npctalk "我饿了。请帮帮我。我饿了。", "", bc_self;
			end;
		case 2:
			erasequest 16105;	// A short break, and a promise
			break;
		}
		switch( checkquest(16113,PLAYTIME) ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "[Fragile Woman]";
			mes "肉……容易消化的肉。饿了。饿了。太好了。肉……";
			mes "我饿了。还想要。好吃。太美味了。";
			next;
			unittalk .@account_id, "" + .@player_name$ + " : Looking at the happiness she gets with " + getitemname(7564) + ".... I feel sorry.", bc_self;
			mes " - ^424242Still... satisfied. Let's come back after dawn.^000000 -";
			close;
		case 2:
			erasequest 16113;	// Endless Hunger - Cooldown
			break;
		}

		.@quest_16111 = checkquest(16111,HUNTING);
		.@quest_16112 = checkquest(16112,HUNTING);
		if (.@quest_16111 == -1 || .@quest_16112 == -1)
			.@state = -1;
		else if (.@quest_16111 == 2 && .@quest_16112 == 2 && countitem(7564) > 9)
			.@state = 2;

		switch( .@state ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "[Fragile Woman]";
			mes "请猎杀乌龟。^0000CD" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_PERMETER", MOB_NAME) + "、" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_SOLIDER", MOB_NAME) + "、" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_FREEZER", MOB_NAME) + "、" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_HEATER", MOB_NAME) + "，每种 10 只。^000000";
			next;
			npctalk "我要死了。死。死。", "", bc_self;
			mes "[Fragile Woman]";
			mes "给我。^0000CD" + getitemname(7564) + "，10 个。^000000";
			mes "拜托。我需要" + getitemname(7564) + "，不然会饿死，太痛苦了。";
			close;
		case 2:
			if (checkweight(1201,1) == 0) {
				mes "[Fragile Woman]";
				mes "你带着这么重的袋子做什么？清理一下吧。";
				close;
			}
			setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, true;
			npctalk "我饿了。请帮帮我。我饿了。", "", bc_self;
			sleep2 500;
			setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
			unittalk .@account_id, "" + .@player_name$ + " : I came back. I finished the hunting.", bc_self;

			mes "[Fragile Woman]";
			mes "啊……啊……肉的香味真好。";
			mes "闻起来真香。看起来好美味。请给我。饿了。快点。";
			next;
			unittalk .@account_id, "" + .@player_name$ + " : ...It smells rotten...", bc_self;
			mes "[Fragile Woman]";
			mes "好吃。太好吃了。好开心。真的很棒。";
			next;
			npctalk "女人：咔嚓咔嚓，嚼……", "", bc_self;
			mes " - ^424242......she is holding it with her bare hands and eating..^000000 -";
			next;
			mes "[Fragile Woman]";
			mes "啊哈，我吃饱了。现在不会死了。我吃饱了。";
			mes "给你这个。不过不能吃……";
			next;
			npctalk "我要死了。死。死。", "", bc_self;
			sleep2 500;
			unittalk .@account_id, "" + .@player_name$ + " : ... " + getitemname(25271) + "?", bc_self;
			mes "[Fragile Woman]";
			mes "我把东西都吃了。抱歉，这是我唯一能给你的。";
			delitem 7564,10;	// Rotten_Meat
			erasequest 16111;	// Endless Hunger 1
			erasequest 16112;	// Endless Hunger 2
			setquest 16113;	// Endless Hunger - Cooldown
			getitem 25271,1;	// IllusionStone
			getexp 1200000,1000000;
			close;
		}
		mes "[Fragile Woman]";
		mes "我饿了。帮帮我。求你。好饿。";
		next;
		unittalk .@account_id, "" + .@player_name$ + " : How.. can I help you?", bc_self;
		mes "[Fragile Woman]";
		mes "...Help me? Really? You won't let me die?";
		next;
		mes "[Fragile Woman]";
		mes "那就请猎杀乌龟。^0000CD" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_PERMETER", MOB_NAME) + "、" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_SOLIDER", MOB_NAME) + "、" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_FREEZER", MOB_NAME) + "、" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_HEATER", MOB_NAME) + "，每种 10 只。^000000";
		mes "请带来肉。" + getitemname(7564) + "，10 个。";
		next;
		npctalk "我饿了。请帮帮我。我饿了。", "", bc_self;
		sleep2 500;
		unittalk .@account_id, "" + .@player_name$ + " : It's all rotten meat.", bc_self;
		mes "[Fragile Woman]";
		mes "拜托。不然我会死。饿死……";
		mes "带来肉。^0000CD" + getitemname(7564) + "，10 个。^000000";
		next;
		mes " - ^424242Archeologist Leiphen couldn't eat... But according to Narin, she will be happy. I can only get the " + getitemname(7564) + " in here.^000000 -";
		next;
		npctalk "我要死了。死。死。", "", bc_self;
		if (select( "I will take it.", "I can't help you now." ) == 2) {
			mes "[Fragile Woman]";
			mes "我要死了。我要饿死了。";
			next;
			mes "[Fragile Woman]";
			mes "^FF0000I can eat anything. Really anything. I can eat.. chomp chomp.^000000";
			close;
		}
		mes "[Fragile Woman]";
		mes "谢谢。没有肉吃我会死。";
		mes "现在我还能再活一阵子。";
		unittalk .@account_id, "" + .@player_name$ + " : ......", bc_self;

		setquest 16112;	// Endless Hunger 2
		close;
	}
	mes "[Fragile Woman]";
	mes "好饿。饿得动不了了……";
	mes "求你……求你发发慈悲。我饿太久了……";
	if (!.talk)
		donpcevent "Fragile Woman#illtur::OnTalk";
	close;

OnTalk:
	.talk = 1;
	switch( rand(2) ) {
	case 0:
		npctalk "我饿了。请帮帮我。我饿了。";
		sleep 3000;
		npctalk "我现在什么都能吃。请给我点东西。";
		break;
	case 1:
		npctalk "我要饿死了。不吃东西我会死。";
		sleep 3000;
		npctalk "我饿了。请帮帮我。求你……";
		break;
	}
	.talk = 0;
	end;

OnInit:
	// cloakonnpc();
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle == 4" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle == 7" );

	// daily
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle > 10 && checkquest(16111,HUNTING) == 2 && checkquest(16112,HUNTING) == 2 && countitem(7564) > 9" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle > 10 && checkquest(16105,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle > 10 && checkquest(16113,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle > 10 && checkquest(16105,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(16113,PLAYTIME) == -1 && isbegin_quest(16111) == 0" );
	end;
}

		setquest 16103;	// The Secret of Strangers
		close;
	}
	if (ill_turtle == 7) {
		npctalk "考古学家 Leiphen：你说最近的地方就在这里附近？我没事了。带路吧！", "", bc_self;
		end;
	}
	if (ill_turtle > 10) {
		switch( isbegin_quest(16106) ) {
		case 0:
			npctalk "考古学家 Leiphen：如果 " + .@player_name$ + " 能安抚纠缠的灵魂，我希望有一天能和它们谈谈。谈谈这里……", "", bc_self;
			end;
		case 1:
			npctalk "考古学家 Leiphen：你好，" + .@player_name$ + "。", "", bc_self;
			mes "[Archeologist Leiphen]";
		mes "你今天又来了。";
			next;
			unittalk .@account_id, "" + .@player_name$ + "：你总是在这里。", bc_self;
			mes "[Archeologist Leiphen]";
		mes "正如我告诉你的，在找到这里的秘密前我不会离开。";
		mes "你是来找 Narin 的吗？";
			next;
			npctalk "考古学家 Leiphen：嗯，吃饭和睡觉的问题在艾尔帕兰解决了。我找到了不错的住处。", "", bc_self;
			sleep2 1000;
			unittalk .@account_id, "" + .@player_name$ + "：你没有课吗？", bc_self;
			mes "[Archeologist Leiphen]";
		mes "呃，我没告诉你。我正在休一年的假。";
			next;
			emotion ET_SWEAT, .@account_id;
			emotion ET_DELIGHT, .@account_id;
			unittalk .@account_id, "" + .@player_name$ + "：好吧……我们一起走？", bc_self;
			mes "[Archeologist Leiphen]";
		mes "走吧！";
			completequest 16106;	// A memory that should not be forgotten 1
			setquest 16107;	// A memory that should not be forgotten 2
			close;
		case 2:
			npctalk "考古学家 Leiphen：你要去龟岛见 Narin 吗？快走吧！", "", bc_self;
			end;
		}
		end;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	cloakonnpc();

	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle == 5" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle == 6 && checkquest(16101,HUNTING) == 2 && checkquest(16102,HUNTING) == 2 && countitem(7564) > 9" );

	// daily
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(16106) == 1" );
	end;
}

		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "你的心情……我甚至无法想象嫂子的心情……唉……你的悲伤……";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "这里的情况解决后，我会去寻找哥哥。他一定独自承受着痛苦和悲伤。";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "我的心也一样痛，但如果你知道老幺正因能力而苦恼……我必须做好准备。谢谢你，冒险者。";
		next;
		ill_luanda = 20;
		getexp 1120000,650000;
		erasequest 5847;
		mes "向露伊娜报告并获得了经验。";
		close;
	}
	if (ill_luanda == 21) {	// SUNDAY vortex 263/72
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "怎么了，冒险者？";
		next;
		mes "向露伊娜讲述了关于陌生人的故事。";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "听起来像是以村子为目标的乌坦或巨石怪物之间的对话。";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "跟随强大的首领？最近我觉得这些家伙的行动有序得令人不安……";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "看来出现了一个领导它们的怪物。";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "真丢脸。即使现在，我们也只能阻挡它们。";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "现在不是悲伤的时候。我们需要加强防御并采取其他措施。";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "强大的首领本身也是问题……但传说中流传下来的力量？我以前从没听过这种故事。";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "我猜这就是怪物攻击村子的原因，但我必须告诉村长并展开调查。";
		next;
		emotion ET_THANKS;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "你刚才讲的故事很有帮助。谢谢你，冒险者。";
		next;
		ill_luanda = 22;
		getexp 1120000,650000;
		erasequest 5847;
		mes "向露伊娜报告并获得了经验。";
		close;
	}
	if (isbegin_quest(5848) == 1 && countitem(25638) > 2) {
		delitem 25638,3;	// Token_Of_Bigstone
		erasequest 5848;
		setquest 5849;
		.@amount = 1;
	}
	if (isbegin_quest(5850) == 1 && countitem(25633) > 2) {
		delitem 25633,3;	// Token_Of_Wootan
		erasequest 5850;
		setquest 5851;
		.@amount += 1;
	}
	if (checkquest(5852,HUNTING) == 2) {
		erasequest 5852;
		setquest 5853;
		.@amount += 1;
	}
	if (checkquest(5854,HUNTING) == 2) {
		erasequest 5854;
		setquest 5855;
		.@amount += 1;
	}
	if (.@amount > 0) {
		getitem 25271, .@amount;	// IllusionStone
		getexp (4300000 * .@amount), (2800000 * .@amount);
		emotion ET_THANKS;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		if (.@amount > 1)
			mes "即使只完成一个请求也很不容易，但你完成了 " + .@amount + " 个……真的很厉害。";
		else
			mes "每一个请求都很重要。一次又一次地感谢你。";
		mes "你辛苦了。";
		close;
	}
	mes "[Luanda]";
	mes "怎么了，冒险者？";
	next;
	switch( select( "这里没有请求吗？", "这个洞窟是什么？", "为什么怪物会入侵？" ) ) {
	case 1:
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "镇上招募的任务有单独的请求板。";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "啊！注意你的精神。这里位于战场中央，所以出于安全考虑，我把请求板设在角落。";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "有时你无法立刻完成请求板上的任务，但没有具体时限，所以不用着急。";
		close;
	case 2:
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "冒险者，你对什么都很好奇。";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "从战略上说，露伊娜洞窟是镇子北边唯一的出口。";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "所以我们在这里防御怪物的攻击。";
		next;
		select( "露伊娜洞窟？露伊娜？" );
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "啊！你注意到了吗？是的，我也叫露伊娜。村里的长者说……";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "据说三胞胎是以村里的洞窟命名的，分别叫卡鲁、露伊娜和毛。";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "我小时候邻居朋友会稍微取笑我，但我不抱怨这个名字。";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "我听说它们是以村子的守护者命名的。我为此感到自豪。";
		close;
	case 3:
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "这对我们来说也非常令人困惑。如果知道原因，我们早就把东西收起来了。";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "这里不是食物充足的城镇，我不认为怪物有什么特别的理由要盯上这里。";
		next;
		mes "[Luanda]";
		mes "但我们能怎么办？我们只是住在这里，而怪物不断来袭，所以只能一边抵挡它们一边生活。";
		close;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(5847) == 1" );
	end;
}


// Shadow npcs and main quest related
com_d02_i,1,1,1	script	com_d02_i_init_shadow_npc	-1,{
	end;
OnInit:
OnHour00:	// updated every 3h at a fixed time
OnHour03:
OnHour06:
OnHour09:
OnHour12:
OnHour15:
OnHour18:
OnHour21:
	setarray .@num[1],1,2,3,4,5,6,7;
	.@today = gettime(DT_DAYOFWEEK);
	.@today = (.@today == 0 ? 7 : .@today);	// SUNDAY - 0
	deletearray .@num[.@today],1;
	deletearray .@num[ rand(1,6) ],1;	// note: should the previous enabled vortex be taken into account?

	for ( .@i = 1; .@i < 6; ++.@i )
		disablenpc "Shimmer#Lu0" + .@num[.@i];
	end;
}

com_d02_i,266,76,6	duplicate(dummy_cloaked_npc)	Silhouette#LuE092	4_GHOST_STAND


// Daily quests
com_d02_i,238,266,4	script	Request Board#Lu	4_BOARD3,{
	if (checkweight(1201,3) == 0) {
		mes "- 你携带的物品太多，无法继续。-";
		close;
	}
	if (ill_luanda == 0) {
		mes "这是写着村庄委托事项的公告板，但现在似乎没有委托。";
		close;
	}

	.@quest_menu$[0] = callsub( S_MenuGather, 5848,5849, "Evidence of eradication of Megaliths" );
	.@quest_menu$[1] = callsub( S_MenuGather, 5850,5851, "Evidence of eradication of Wootan" );
	.@quest_menu$[2] = callsub( S_MenuHunt, 5852,5853, "Operation to eradicate Megaliths" );
	.@quest_menu$[3] = callsub( S_MenuHunt, 5854,5855, "Operation to eradicate Wootan" );

	.@playtime_5849 = checkquest(5849,PLAYTIME);
	.@playtime_5851 = checkquest(5851,PLAYTIME);
	.@playtime_5853 = checkquest(5853,PLAYTIME);
	.@playtime_5855 = checkquest(5855,PLAYTIME);

	// menu to take all quests at once
	if (.@playtime_5849 > 0 || .@playtime_5851 > 0 || .@playtime_5853 > 0 || .@playtime_5855 > 0) {	// at least one of the quests is in cooldown
		.@quest_menu$[4] = "^777777[Waiting] Receive all quests^000000";
		.@all_quests = 0;
	}
	else if (isbegin_quest(5848) > 0 || isbegin_quest(5850) > 0 || isbegin_quest(5852) > 0 || isbegin_quest(5854) > 0) {	// at least one of the quests is in progress
		.@quest_menu$[4] = "^777777[Progress] Receive all quests^000000";
		.@all_quests = 1;
	}
	else {	// can take all quests
		.@quest_menu$[4] = "[Daily] Accept all quests";
		.@all_quests = 2;
	}

	switch( select( .@quest_menu$[0], .@quest_menu$[1], .@quest_menu$[2], .@quest_menu$[3], .@quest_menu$[4] ) ) {
	case 1:
		switch( .@playtime_5849 ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
		mes "这项委托明天可以再次完成。";
			close;
		case 2:
			break;
		}
		switch( isbegin_quest(5848) ) {
		case 0:
		mes "村庄正被巨石魔怪搞得焦头烂额。如果你带来3个" + getitemname(25638) + "作为击败巨石魔怪的证明，我会奖励你。";
			mes "  ";
		mes "首领";
			next;
			if (select( "接受委托", "不接受" ) == 2)
				end;
			setquest 5848;
			open_quest_ui(5848);
			mes "已收到你的委托。请向^FF0000防御管理者卢安达^000000报告。";
			close;
		case 1:
		case 2:
			// note: the amount of item is not checked
			mes "^FF0000巨石消灭证明^000000";
			mes "你的委托正在进行中。";
			mes "你可以取消。但取消后目前进度会丢失。确定要取消吗？";
			next;
			if (select( "不取消", "取消" ) == 1)
				end;
			erasequest 5848;
			mes "委托已取消。";
			close;
		}
		end;
	case 2:
		switch( .@playtime_5851 ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "这个任务明天可以再次进行。";
			close;
		case 2:
			break;
		}
		switch( isbegin_quest(5850) ) {
		case 0:
			mes "乌坦族给村庄造成了很多麻烦。带来 3 个" + getitemname(25633) + "证明击败了他们，我会给你奖励。";
			mes "  ";
			mes "首领";
			next;
			if (select( "Accept request", "Do not accept" ) == 2)
				end;
			setquest 5850;
			open_quest_ui(5850);
			mes "已收到你的委托。请向^FF0000防御管理者卢安达^000000报告。";
			close;
		case 1:
		case 2:
			// note: the amount of item is not checked
			mes "^FF0000乌坦族消灭证明^000000";
			mes "你的委托正在进行中。";
			mes "你可以取消。但取消后目前进度会丢失。确定要取消吗？";
			next;
			if (select( "Do not cancel", "Cancel" ) == 1)
				end;
			erasequest 5850;
			mes "委托已取消。";
			close;
		}
		end;
	case 3:
		switch( .@playtime_5853 ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "这个任务明天可以再次进行。";
			close;
		case 2:
			break;
		}
		switch( checkquest(5852,HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			mes "我们计划讨伐巨石。能捕获 5 只" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_TRI_JOINT",MOB_NAME) + "、" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_STALACTIC_GOLEM",MOB_NAME) + "和" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_MEGALITH",MOB_NAME) + "的，请申请。";
			mes "  ";
			mes "首领";
			next;
			if (select( "Accept request", "Do not accept" ) == 2)
				end;
			setquest 5852;
			open_quest_ui(5852);
			mes "已收到你的委托。请向^FF0000防御管理者卢安达^000000报告。";
			close;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "^FF0000巨石讨伐^000000";
			mes "你的委托正在进行中。";
			mes "你可以取消。但取消后目前进度会丢失。确定要取消吗？";
			next;
			if (select( "Do not cancel", "Cancel" ) == 1)
				end;
			erasequest 5852;
			mes "委托已取消。";
			close;
		case 2:
			mes "可以向防御管理者卢安达领取这个任务的奖励。";
			close;
		}
		end;
	case 4:
		switch( .@playtime_5855 ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "这个任务明天可以再次进行。";
			close;
		case 2:
			break;
		}
		switch( checkquest(5854,HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			mes "我们计划讨伐乌坦族。能捕获 5 只" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_STONE_SHOOTER",MOB_NAME) + "、" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_WOOTAN_SHOOTER",MOB_NAME) + "和" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_WOOTAN_FIGHTER",MOB_NAME) + "的，请申请。";
			mes "  ";
			mes "首领";
			next;
			if (select( "Accept request", "Do not accept" ) == 2)
				end;
			setquest 5854;
			open_quest_ui(5854);
			mes "已收到你的委托。请向^FF0000防御管理者卢安达^000000报告。";
			close;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "^FF0000Evidence of eradication of megaliths^000000";
			mes "你的委托正在进行中。";
			mes "你可以取消。但取消后目前进度会丢失。确定要取消吗？";
			next;
			if (select( "Do not cancel", "Cancel" ) == 1)
				end;
			erasequest 5854;
			mes "委托已取消。";
			close;
		case 2:
			mes "可以向防御管理者卢安达领取这个任务的奖励。";
			close;
		}
		end;
	case 5:
		switch( .@all_quests ) {
		case 0:	// at least one of the quests is in cooldown
			mes "有任务仍在冷却中。";
			close;
		case 1:	// at least one of the quests is in progress
			mes "有些任务正在进行中。";
			close;
		case 2:	// can take all quests
			mes "确定要接受任务看板上的所有任务吗？";
			next;
			if (select( "Accept", "Do not accept" ) == 2)
				end;
			setquest 5848;
			setquest 5850;
			setquest 5852;
			setquest 5854;
			mes "任务看板上的所有任务都已接受。";
			close;
		}
		end;
	}
	end;

S_MenuGather:
	.@string$ = getarg(2);

	switch( checkquest(getarg(1),PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		switch( isbegin_quest(getarg(0)) ) {
		case 0:
			.@menu$ = "[Daily] " + .@string$;
			break;
		case 1:
		case 2:
			// note: the amount of item is not checked
			.@menu$ = "^777777[Progress] " + .@string$ + "^000000";
			break;
		}
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		.@menu$ = "^777777[Waiting]" + .@string$ + "^000000";
		break;
	case 2:
		erasequest getarg(1);
		.@menu$ = "[Daily]" + .@string$;
		break;
	}
	return .@menu$;

S_MenuHunt:
	.@string$ = getarg(2);

	switch( checkquest(getarg(1),PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		switch( checkquest(getarg(0),HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			.@menu$ = "[Daily] " + .@string$;
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			.@menu$ = "^777777[Progress] " + .@string$ + "^000000";
			break;
		case 2:
			.@menu$ = "^777777[Reward] " + .@string$ + "^000000";
			break;
		}
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		.@menu$ = "^777777[Waiting] " + .@string$ + "^000000";
		break;
	case 2:
		erasequest getarg(1);
		.@menu$ = "[Daily] " + .@string$;
		break;
	}
	return .@menu$;
}

	.@menu$[1] = callsub( S_Quest, "消灭深海鱼人", 3420,3419 );

			break;

			break;

		case -1:
			mes "[Kena]";
			mes "请各击杀 10 只^4d4dff" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_SWORD_FISH", MOB_NAME) + "^000000、^4d4dff" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_PHEN", MOB_NAME) + "^000000和^4d4dff" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_KING_DRAMOH", MOB_NAME) + "^000000。";

			close2;
			setquest 3417;
			end;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "[Kena]";
			mes "^4d4dff" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_SWORD_FISH", MOB_NAME) + "^000000、^4d4dff" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_PHEN", MOB_NAME) + "^000000和^4d4dff" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_KING_DRAMOH", MOB_NAME) + "^000000。";

			mes "请各击杀 10 只。";
			close;
		case 2:
			mes "[Kena]";
			mes "它们都已经被消灭了，辛苦你了。";
			next;
			mes "[Kena]";
			mes "有时间的话请明天再来。我会等你的。";
			close2;
			getitem 25271,1;	// IllusionStone
			getexp 20107500,14075250;
			erasequest 3417;
			setquest 3418;
			end;
		}
		end;
	case 2:
		switch( checkquest(3420,PLAYTIME) ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "[凯娜]";
			mes "我想今天已经够了，请明天再来。";
			close;
		case 2:
			erasequest 3420;
			break;
		}
		switch( checkquest(3419,HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			mes "[凯娜]";
			mes "请各消灭15只^4d4dff" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_SEDORA", MOB_NAME) + "^000000和^4d4dff" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_STROUF", MOB_NAME) + "^000000。";
			next;
			mes "[凯娜]";
			mes "它们很烦人，请小心。";
			close2;
			setquest 3419;
			end;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "[凯娜]";
			mes "请各消灭15只^4d4dff" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_SEDORA", MOB_NAME) + "^000000和^4d4dff" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_STROUF", MOB_NAME) + "^000000。";
			close;
		case 2:
			mes "[凯娜]";
			mes "它们都被消灭了，谢谢你的努力。";
			next;
			mes "[凯娜]";
			mes "时间合适时请明天再来。我会等着你。";
			close2;
			getitem 25271,1;	// IllusionStone
			getexp 20107500,14075250;
			setquest 3420;
			erasequest 3419;
			end;
		}
		end;
	case 3:
		mes "[凯娜]";
		mes "糟糕……";
		next;
		mes "[凯娜]";
		mes "放着不管会很危险……";
		close;
	}
	end;

S_Quest:
	switch( checkquest(getarg(1),PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		switch( checkquest(getarg(2),HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			.@string$ = getarg(0);
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			.@string$ = getarg(0) + " - In Progress";
			break;
		case 2:
			.@string$ = getarg(0) + " - Done";
			break;
		}
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		.@string$ = "^999999" + getarg(0) + " - Pending^000000";
		break;
	case 2:
		.@string$ = getarg(0);
		break;
	}
	return .@string$;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(3418,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(3417,HUNTING) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(3417,HUNTING) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(3418,PLAYTIME) == 2" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(3420,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(3419,HUNTING) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(3419,HUNTING) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(3420,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	end;
}

ein_d02_i,168,206,5	script	Gloomy Teddy Bear#ITB	4_TEDDY_BEAR_B_L,{
	if (checkweight(1201,3) == 0) {
		mes "- 等一下！！";
		mes "- 你携带的物品太多。-";
		mes "- 无法领取。-";
		mes "- 减轻负重后-";
		mes "- 再试。-";
		close;
	}
	if (ill_teddy < 9) {
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "啊……";
		mes "这是梦还是现实……";
		mes "我是死了……还是活着……";
		next;
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "我们……不该回来……";
		mes "我必须回去……";
		close;
	}
	switch( checkquest(11569,PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "哎呀……别那样催我……";
		close;
	case 2:
		erasequest 11569;
		break;
	}
	switch( checkquest(11567,HUNTING) ) {
	case -1:
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "Ah...";
		mes "这是梦还是现实……";
		mes "我是死了……还是活着……";
		next;
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "我们……不该回来……";
		mes "我必须回去……";
		next;
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "嘿……别站在那里，帮帮我……";
		mes "我……纳撒里安人……";
		next;
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "^ff0000击杀10只" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_TEDDY_BEAR_R", MOB_NAME) + "、" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_TEDDY_BEAR_Y", MOB_NAME) + "、" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_TEDDY_BEAR_G", MOB_NAME) + "、" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_TEDDY_BEAR_B", MOB_NAME) + "和" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_TEDDY_BEAR_W", MOB_NAME) + "^000000，送它们回深处……安息……";
		next;
		if (select( "接受", "放弃" ) == 2) {
			mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
			mes "...Ah...";
			close;
		}
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "我想结束这场……地狱般的噩梦……";
		mes "来吧……";
		setquest 11567;
		setquest 11568;
		close;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "啊……平静的安息何时到来……";
		mes "……请求还没完成吗……";
		close;
	case 2:
		switch( checkquest(11568,HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			end;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
			mes "啊……平静的安息何时到来……";
			mes "……请求还没完成吗……";
			close;
		case 2:
			mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
			mes "为什么它还……没有消失……";
			mes "这场可怕的噩梦……";
			erasequest 11567;
			erasequest 11568;
			setquest 11569;
			getitem 25271,1; // IllusionStone
			getexp 1300000,1000000;
			close;
		}
		end;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_teddy == 9 && checkquest(11569,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(11567,HUNTING) == -1 && checkquest(11568,HUNTING) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(11569,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(11567,HUNTING) == 2 && checkquest(11568,HUNTING) == 2" );
	end;
}


ein_d02_i,191,192,3	script	Porcelain Doll Craftsman#ITB	4_F_HUWOMAN,{
	if (checkweight(1201,3) == 0) {
		mes "- 等一下！！";
		mes "- 你携带的物品太多。-";
		mes "- 无法领取。-";
		mes "- 减轻负重后-";
		mes "- 再试。-";
		close;
	}
	if (ill_teddy < 9) {
		mes "[Porcelain Doll Craftsman]";
		mes "呀啊啊啊啊！！！！！";
		mes "吓到你了！";
		close;
	}
	switch( checkquest(11571,PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[Porcelain Doll Craftsman]";
		mes "冒险者，欢迎回来。";
		mes "多亏了你，我才能继续在矿井深处研究。";
		mes "黎明时再回来吧。";
		close;
	case 2:
		erasequest 11571;
		break;
	}
	switch( checkquest(11570,HUNTING) ) {
	case -1:
		mes "[Porcelain Doll Craftsman]";
		mes "呀啊啊啊啊！！！！！";
		mes "吓到你了！";
		next;
		mes "[Porcelain Doll Craftsman]";
		mes "你在附近见过巨大的^ff0000" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_TEDDY_BEAR_S", MOB_NAME) + "^000000吗？";
		mes "我好不容易到了入口，但每次想离开它就出现，已经藏在这里好几天了。";
		next;
		if (select( "帮助", "放弃" ) == 2) {
			mes "[Porcelain Doll Craftsman]";
			mes "呜呜！";
			mes "完了！！！";
			mes "我要永远困在这里了！";
			close;
		}
		mes "[Porcelain Doll Craftsman]";
		mes "还活着！！！";
		mes "你看到了吗？";
		mes "是一只巨大的^ff0000" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_TEDDY_BEAR_S", MOB_NAME) + "^000000。";
		mes "你必须杀了它！";
		setquest 11570;
		close;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[Porcelain Doll Craftsman]";
		mes "你好吗？";
		mes "你杀死^ff0000" + getmonsterinfo("ILL_TEDDY_BEAR_S", MOB_NAME) + "^000000了吗？";
		close;
	case 2:
		// unknown text
		erasequest 11570;
		setquest 11571;
		getitem 25271,1; // IllusionStone
		getexp 1500000,1100000;
		end;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_teddy == 9 && checkquest(11571,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(11570,HUNTING) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(11571,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(11570,HUNTING) == 2" );
	end;
}
